\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{m}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

holds for any
{w}_{1}\in W_{1}
,
i\in\mathbb{N}
and
m\in\mathbb{Z}
.
We show this in several different ranges of
m
.

When
m\in\mathbb{N}
, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{m}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}(\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{x})^{m}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{2+i}L(\bm{\zeta}_{1})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

by changing the variables as
\bm{\zeta}_{1}=\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x}
.
Note that the formal series

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{x})^{m}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{2+i}\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L(\bm{\zeta}_{1})\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}

does not involve any negative powers of
\bm{\zeta}_{1}
,
and hence its residue with respect to
\bm{\zeta}_{1}
vanishes.
Therefore we can subtract the residue for free to obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{m}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}(\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{x})^{m}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{2+i}L(\bm{\zeta}_{1})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)-\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}(\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{x})^{m}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{2+i}\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L(\bm{\zeta}_{1})\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}(\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{x})^{m}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{2+i}\bm{x}^{-1}\delta\left(\frac{\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{\zeta}_{0}}{\bm{x}}\right)\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}},

where we used the commutativity (Lemma
B.9
) in the last equality.
We also use the identity

\bm{x}^{-1}\delta\left(\frac{\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{\zeta}_{0}}{\bm{x}}\right)=\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{-1}\delta\left(\frac{\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0}}{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}\right)

to obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{m}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}(\bm{\zeta}_{1}-\bm{x})^{m}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{2+i}\bm{\zeta}_{1}^{-1}\delta\left(\frac{\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0}}{\bm{\zeta}_{1}}\right)\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{m}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}.

This in particular implies (
B.6
) in the case when
m\in\mathbb{N}
.

We show (
B.6
) when
m<0
by induction.
When
m=-1
, we see that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-1}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{r=0}^{i}\binom{i}{r}\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{r-1}\bm{x}^{i-r}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right).

We divide the sum into the part of
r=0
and those of
r=1,\dots,i
. For the former case, we apply Lemma
B.11
,
and for the latter cases, we can already apply (
B.6
) to obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-1}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{r=0}^{i}\binom{i}{r}\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{r-1}\bm{x}^{i-r}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-1}(\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0})^{2+j}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0})u^{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right).

Therefore (
B.6
) holds for
m\geq-1
.

Suppose that
k\in\mathbb{Z}_{>0}
is such that (
B.6
) holds for
m\geq-k
.
In particular, we have

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

for any
{w}_{1}\in W_{1}
, and
i\in\mathbb{N}
.
We differentiate both sides in terms of
\bm{x}
.
The derivative of the left hand side (LHS) becomes

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\bm{x}}(\mathrm{LHS})

\displaystyle\;=\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}\left(\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\right)\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L_{-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\;=k\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k-1}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L_{-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right).

On the other hand, the derivative of the right hand side (RHS) is

\displaystyle\frac{d}{d\bm{x}}(\mathrm{RHS})

\displaystyle\;=\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}\left(\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\right)\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L_{-1}L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\;=k\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k-1}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0})L_{-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right).

Here we used the commutation relation (
B.4
) at
n=-1
in the last equality.
Comparing these, due to the induction hypothesis, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k-1}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\mathrm{Res}_{\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{-k-1}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{2+i}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(\bar{w}_{c,h_{\infty}},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes\bar{w}_{c,h_{0}}\right).

Hence, (
B.6
) holds also at
m=-k-1
.

In conclusion, we have shown that (
B.6
)
holds for arbitrary
{w}_{1}\in W_{1}
,
i\in\mathbb{N}
and
m\in\mathbb{Z}
,
which is equivalent to the desired result.
∎

Proof of Proposition
B.10
.

We introduce another filtration on
W_{0}
as

G^{d}W_{0}:=\bigoplus_{k=0}^{d}W_{0}(k),\quad d\in\mathbb{N}.

In particular, each subspace
G^{d}W_{0}
is finite dimensional.

For
d,r\in\mathbb{N}
, we name the following statement:

\mathbf{P}[d;r]
: There exists
k\in\mathbb{N}
depending on
d
such that

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}))

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0})

holds for any
{w}_{0}\in G^{d}W_{0}
,
{w}_{1}\in W_{1}
and
{w}_{\infty}\in\mathscr{F}^{r}W_{\infty}
.

To prove Proposition
B.10
, we employ the induction in
p,r\in\mathbb{N}
.
The statement
\mathbf{P}[0;0]
is true due to Lemma
B.12
.

Assume that
\mathbf{P}[d;r]
is true.
For
{w}_{0}\in G^{d}W_{0}
,
{w}_{1}\in W_{1}
,
{w}_{\infty}\in\mathscr{F}^{r}W_{\infty}
and
n>0
such that
L_{-n}{w}_{0}\in G^{d+1}W_{0}
, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L_{-n}{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})L_{-n}\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L_{k-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right).

Using the commutation relation (
B.4
), we get

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L_{-n}{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{n}{w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{-n+1}\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2(-n+1)(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{-n}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{c}{2}\binom{-n+1}{3}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{-n-2}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L_{k-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right).

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes L_{-n}{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{n}{w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)-\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\left(L_{-n}\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{n}{w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L_{k-1}L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{n}{w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0})L_{k-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\Biggl(\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{k}\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+2k\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{k-1}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{c}{2}\binom{k}{3}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{k-3}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\Biggr).

Using the identities

(B.7)

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\binom{k}{j}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\bm{\zeta}_{0}^{k-j}

\displaystyle\;=\binom{-n+1}{j}(\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0})^{-n+1-j},\quad j\in\mathbb{N},

we obtain

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes L_{-n}{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{n}{w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{-n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{-n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0})L_{k-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-(\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0})^{-n+1}\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-2(-n+1)(\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0})^{-n}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{c}{2}\binom{-n+1}{3}(\bm{x}+\bm{\zeta}_{0})^{-n-2}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right).

By induction hypothesis
\mathbf{P}[d;r]
, there exists
m\in\mathbb{N}
depending on
d
and
n
such that

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{m}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L_{-n}{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{m}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes L_{-n}{w}_{0}\right).

Since
G^{d+1}W_{0}
is finite dimensional, we can maximize such
m
so that it depends only on
d+1
,
implying that
\mathbf{P}[d+1;r]
is also true.

Next, we apply
L_{-n}
with
n>0
at
{w}_{\infty}\in\mathscr{F}^{r}W_{\infty}
.
On one hand, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{-n}{w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L_{k-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L_{n}{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n+1}\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2(n+1)(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c}{2}\binom{n+1}{3}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n-2}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right).

Note that the rational function
\binom{n+1}{3}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n-2}

appearing in the last line is a polynomial of
\bm{\zeta}_{0}
and
\bm{x}
for all
n>0
.
On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{-n}{w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\;=

\displaystyle\sum_{k=0}^{\infty}\binom{n+1}{k}\bm{x}^{n+1-k}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0})L_{k-1}{w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n+1}\frac{{\partial}}{{\partial}\bm{\zeta}_{0}}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+2(n+1)(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{c}{2}\binom{n+1}{3}(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{n-2}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

\displaystyle\quad+\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left({w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes L_{n}{w}_{0}\right).

Here we again used the identities from (
B.7
).
Since
L_{n}{w}_{0}\in G^{d}W_{0}
, we can apply the induction hypothesis
\mathbf{P}[d;r]

to conclude that there exists
m\in\mathbb{N}
depending on
d
such that

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{m}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{-n}{w}_{\infty},L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})\left(\mathcal{Y}_{t}({w}_{1},\bm{x})\otimes{w}_{0}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=\;

\displaystyle(\bm{\zeta}_{0}+\bm{x})^{m}\widehat{\mathbb{M}}\left(L_{-n}{w}_{\infty},\mathcal{Y}_{t}\left(L(\bm{\zeta}_{0}){w}_{1},\bm{x}\right)\otimes{w}_{0}\right)

holds, implying that
\mathbf{P}[d;r+1]
is also true.
∎

Proof of Proposition
B.8
.

The Jacobi identity is equivalent to having
both of the commutativity (Lemma
B.9
) and the associativity (Proposition
B.10
).
In
[
LL04
]
, this equivalence is shown only for module maps,
but the same arguments work for intertwining operators.
∎
